# Astrographer — Active Decisions Summary

Maintained by the document-archival loop (AGENTS.md item 6). ACTIVE =
governs the current project; SUPERSEDED = replaced (provenance only).
Decisions are recorded as `DECIDED:` / `ACTIVE` / `SUPERSEDED` rows and kept
in sync with `docs/specs/*.md`. Imported constraints from the foundation /
upstream project are marked **IMPORTED** (their canonical record is in
`../Provident-Electron/docs/decisions.md` / `../Preempt-Providence/docs/`).

## ACTIVE

| Decision | Date | What it pins | Source |

| **DECIDED: LIVE-GATE-RUN-DISCIPLINE — a live run must use ISOLATED PORTS and confirm boot-and-connect before driving; the mandatory pre-live leg's BOOT precondition (the thing `A-7` exists to guarantee) is what bars a battery, so a leg red at the DRIVE step with boot demonstrably GREEN does not bar it IF the decision and its reason are recorded; a live PASS is ADDITIVE and APP-green ONLY and retires no `[T]`/node row; a matrix report with no verdict per declared row is INVALID; and a live FAIL is EVIDENCE split by layer before any disposition** | 2026-09-29 (the supervisor's tracker/landing pass, recording the standing discipline established by the program's **FIRST REAL-ELECTRON LIVE BATTERY** and by the harness unit that made it possible — `U-DIVERGENCE-SPAWN`, its DONE row and the `PD-UI-6` gate-6 addendum at the head of `docs/next-steps.md`'s `CURRENT WORK / handover-state` region; the pass record is this pass's tracker rows: `docs/next-steps.md`, `docs/defects.md`, `docs/pending.md`; **the readings below are RECORDED READINGS of the live-scenario runner and the supervisor, labelled at their sites — this pass ran no leg**) | **(i) ISOLATED PORTS, AND BOOT-AND-CONNECT CONFIRMED BEFORE DRIVING.** A live run uses **its own MCP port and its own CDP port** and confirms boot-and-connect before any block is driven. **The reason it is a rule and not hygiene:** the first run of this pass's battery was **TRUNCATED AT BLOCK 82** by a **concurrent sibling process** that held CDP **`:9222`** and issued **`pkill -f "electron \."`** — the sibling's `pkill` killed the run's own Electron; the re-run on **`--port=3899 --cdp-port=9333`** completed (`done: 100 blocks, 39 FAIL, 3 PARKED`; block split PASS 31 / FAIL 39 / PARK 3 / DIAG 27). **The hazard is standing, not incidental** (`docs/pending.md` §DEFERRED: the concurrent sibling holds `:9222` and issues `pkill -f "electron \."`, so **every** live gate must use isolated ports), and the boot confirmation is what makes a later FAIL attributable: the completed run's boot read **`[provident-mcp] http transport ready on http://127.0.0.1:3899/mcp`** · **`[provident-main] renderer ready — MCP backend armed`** · **`DevTools listening on ws://127.0.0.1:9333/…`** with **no `/dev/shm` / `SIGTRAP` match** — so the 39 FAILs are **APP-layer evidence, not boot noise**. **(ii) THE DISCRIMINATOR IS *WHERE* THE PRE-LIVE LEG IS RED — AND THE RULE IS THE `A-7` DISPOSITION'S OWN LOGIC APPLIED TO THE LEG.** The mandatory pre-live leg's **BOOT** half is the precondition `A-7` exists to guarantee (`A-7` = the program's **PRE-LIVE LEG BOOT precondition** disposition, read through `docs/decisions.md`; `U-DIVERGENCE-SPAWN`'s whole subject is *"the divergence-leg spawn precondition … the boot-success census, and the fail-loud rule"*, `docs/specs/unit-divergence-harness-precondition.md`). Therefore **a leg red at the DRIVE step, with its boot demonstrably green, does not bar a live battery** — **and this pass USED the rule**: the legs at this head report **`[provident-mcp] stdio transport ready`** · **`[provident-main] renderer ready — MCP backend armed`**, **zero** `/dev/shm` / `shared memory` / `SIGTRAP` matches, and the scratch cleanup **`removed 2 · passes 3 · leftover: NONE`** with 0 surviving processes — **while the leg still EXITS `1`, for a NEW and different reason: a DRIVE-step fixture mismatch** (`provident.dispatch` → `isError` text `unresolved target: {"kind":"cssId","cssId":"inc"}`, because leg 1's drive targets the leg's own demo-envelope fixture while the app's boot wiring serves its own `#wiki-root`/`zone:main` template). **WHAT IS *NOT* LICENSED:** (a) the rule covers **the DRIVE step only** — an **environmental/precondition FAILURE** such as the recorded `Creating shared memory in /dev/shm/… failed: Permission denied (13)` → `exited with signal SIGTRAP`, which surfaced only as the generic `electron connect/drive failed: MCP error -32000: Connection closed`, **DID** bar the battery and is exactly why the earlier units' live layers read `PRECONDITION-FAILED`; (b) a **boot-red** reading, in any wording, **never** converts into a battery; (c) the reason must be **printed in the same record as the battery** — a red leg passed over silently is the failure mode this clause exists to close. **(iii) A LIVE PASS IS ADDITIVE AND APP-GREEN ONLY — IT RETIRES NO `[T]`/NODE ROW.** `PD-UI-6`'s four live claims `L-1`..`L-4` plus its landed class-XOR re-pin now read **`LIVE PASS (ADDITIVE / APP-GREEN ONLY)`** (identical across two runs; `exactlyOneOf(.is-open / .is-closed)` TRUE before the open, inside the open, and after the close), and that reading **retires no envelope row, no node row and no trio row** — the two layers are **independent evidence about the same unit**, never substitutes: `RCA-12`'s *"a node-suite green is ENVELOPE-green, not APP-green"* runs in both directions, so an app-green is **not** envelope evidence either. **A live PASS makes no claim beyond the rows it reads.** **(iv) A MATRIX REPORT THAT DOES NOT RETURN A VERDICT PER DECLARED ROW IS INVALID — AND `OK` IS NOT A PASS.** The first battery's `§5.U` matrix report returned **only `2 of 8`** declared rows (`U-1`, `U-7`) while the mapped blocks reported `UF-*` ids, so **`U-2`..`U-6` and `U-8` carry NO `U-*` verdict**, and **`reconcileMatrixRows` still printed `OK` in full-battery mode**. Comply: **`OK` from a matrix leg may not be read as a pass until the report returns a verdict for every declared row**; *"the instrument printed OK"* and *"every declared row has a verdict"* are two different statements. **A partial matrix report is INVALID evidence** — neither a pass nor a fail — and the instrument's failure to refuse **is itself a defect row** (`docs/defects.md`; owed to the live-driver's owning unit). **(v) A LIVE FAIL IS EVIDENCE — SPLIT BY LAYER BEFORE ANY DISPOSITION.** The 39 FAILs of the first battery are recorded **by group, not as a blanket claim: 32 APP-layer `[U]` · 2 DRIVER/scenario · 5 FIXTURE/precondition** (`docs/defects.md`), each group with its layer and its owner, because **the three have different owners and different fix shapes**: an APP FAIL is a unit's own work; a DRIVER FAIL (a scenario asserting `/editingMode:(contenteditable\|textarea)/` against an app that renders `representationMode: html\|markdown`, or a FAIL carrying an **EMPTY evidence field**) is the live-driver's; a FIXTURE FAIL (the `gnosis_*` rows with **no engine running** — `connect ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:8080` — or the `o0_*` diagnostics' census mismatch, claimed 226 docs vs 2 observed) is the harness/engine unit's. **A layer-blind FAIL count is not a finding**, and a driver or fixture FAIL **must never be dispositioned as an app defect** (nor the reverse). | `scripts/live-drive.mjs` (the live-scenario runner) and its run reading; `scripts/electron-divergence.mjs` + `docs/specs/unit-divergence-harness-precondition.md` (`U-DIVERGENCE-SPAWN`); the `PD-UI-6` gate-6 reading and its unit spec `docs/specs/unit-pd-ui-6-modal-state.md` `§7.2` (`L-1`..`L-4`); `docs/specs/ci-divergence-leg.md` + `docs/specs/unit-o-0-per-stage-measurement.md` (the leg's `A-7` boot-precondition discipline); `docs/defects.md` (this pass's live-battery rows) · **Tests that pin it:** the live driver's own contract suite `tests/live-drive-contract.test.ts` (the driver's rule surface); the leg's contract `tests/unit-divergence-spawn-contract.test.ts` (register `held × 8`, `75 = 20+6+5+12+12+8+6+6` executed == declared) |
| **DECIDED: POST-DIVISION-REBUILD-FOUNDATION-PIN-REFRESH-AND-STANDING-REPIN-POLICY — the pin is the manifest's `foundation.commit` = the foundation's CURRENT HEAD; a move of that HEAD is a PIN FAULT BY DESIGN and is met by ONE FOUR-SITE ATOMIC RESTATEMENT in its own unit (never a carry, never a partial re-statement, never a revision-tolerant arm)** | 2026-09-28 (the `PD-VENDOR-PIN-REFRESH` unit's `§8 D-8` decision, ordered by `§9 E-2` and `§12.2` and printed there verbatim so it could not drift; verified ABSENT by that unit's item-10d documentation review (`archive/reviews/2026-09-28-pin-refresh-doc-review.md`) and by its gate-4 audit (finding `A-2`, BLOCKING). Recorded by the supervisor's landing pass; the unit contract is `docs/specs/unit-pd-vendor-pin-refresh.md`; the pin's founding row is `DECIDED: POST-DIVISION-REBUILD-VENDORING-AND-PHASE-SHAPE` clause (1)'s mechanical-pin model) | **What it pins.** **(a) THE PIN'S VALUE AND ITS RULE.** The pin is **`vendor/foundation.lock.json`'s `foundation.commit`**, and it **MUST equal the adjacent foundation tree's CURRENT HEAD** — at this landing **`d7b98b574adc7fa63fbabda617eba2a753f52cb5`** (the docs-only commit *"Repair the overlay fork/guide miscommunication a downstream consumer hit (Astrographer OS-1)"*; **no `src/**` byte moved**, and the fifteen vendored modules are **byte-identical at both revisions** — identical blob object-ids for all fifteen over `src/shared`, and `git diff --name-only 8f193a8d… d7b98b5… -- src/shared` **EMPTY**; measurer: **the supervisor**, a RECORDED READING). **The literal is a READING, not a permanent constant** (`§8 D-2`): whichever HEAD the refresh reads is what it pins. **(b) THE FOUR-SITE ATOMIC RESTATEMENT RULE.** A refresh moves **FOUR sites TOGETHER, in ONE commit**: **(1)** the manifest's `foundation.commit`; **(2)** the **embedded `digestCommand` literal** inside the manifest; **(3)** and **(4)** the **three `PINNED_COMMIT` constants** — `tests/pd-vendor-set.test.ts` · `tests/pd-vendor-manifest.test.ts` · `tests/pd-vendor-drift.test.ts`, one declaration each. **A PARTIAL RESTATEMENT IS THE FAILURE MODE, NOT A SHORTCUT** — it is exactly what the register's row **`P-SM-pd-pin-2`** exists to catch (`F-1`/`F-2`), and **what must NOT move with them** is equally pinned: **no module byte, no per-module `md5`, no `lineCount`** (`§8 D-1`; `§8 D-4`) — a changed digest is the refresh's **STOP** condition, not a result. **(c) THE STANDING POLICY FOR FUTURE UPSTREAM HEAD MOVES** (`§9 E-2`, now a RULE rather than prose): **every** future foundation HEAD move is **a pin fault by design**; **each opens its own unit (or an explicitly-scoped row of the pin's owning unit)**; **each re-states all four sites atomically**; **each re-takes the `§3` item 3 PREMISE CHECK BEFORE editing** (per-module blob object-ids at both revisions — the check that makes a refresh a refresh rather than a re-vendor); and **each re-reads `npm run drift` to `CLEAN`**. **A pass that re-pins silently to make a monitor green, without the premise check, is the exact false-green the pin exists to prevent.** **(d) THE NEVER-MAKE-THE-ARM-REVISION-TOLERANT RULE — A RE-AFFIRMATION OF `D-3`.** The pin arm gates on a **successful `rev-parse`** and the comparator **refuses `CLEAN` BEFORE it compares bytes** (the pin difference is pushed first, so equal bytes can never outvote a moved revision): **a byte-equal tree at another commit is a PIN FAULT and NEVER `CLEAN`**, exit `1`. **The arm may NEVER be dropped, loosened, hard-coded green, or made revision-tolerant** — it is **precisely the false-green the `PD-VENDOR` adversarial pass constructed** (*"THE PIN IS A RECORD, NOT AN INSTRUMENT"*) and that the **blind-greens pass independently falsified** by building a `git init` tree at **another revision with equal bytes** and reading `FAIL … equal bytes at a different commit are NOT the pinned state`, **exit `1`, NO `CLEAN`**. **Loosening it is the one change that would undo the unit's reason for existing** (`§2 F-4`). **THE OTHER SIDE OF THE SAME MECHANISM, named so it is not confused with this rule:** a `rev-parse` that **FAILS** for a **present** tree leaves a **byte-only `CLEAN`** — that is the **INSTRUMENT SEAM** filed as its own gate **`PD-VENDOR-DRIFT-REVISION-STATUS`** (`docs/pending.md`; `§9 E-9`), **NOT a revision-tolerant arm**, and **fixing it is that gate's, never a refresh's**. **(e) WHAT THIS ROW DOES *NOT* DO:** it changes **no vendored byte**, re-measures **nothing**, and settles **no** other owed item (`E-10`'s `baselines` cells and `E-11`'s `provenance` wording stay owed; `docs/pending.md`). **Authority (read, never modified):** `../Provident-Electron/` is **readable and never modifiable** (`§8 D-7`). |
| **DECIDED: POST-DIVISION-REBUILD-PD-UI-6-ADAPTER-CONTRACTS — the modal adapter's THREE behaviour-pinning clauses that derive from NO earlier row: (i) the Escape route binds the verb `'escape'` and invokes the callback exactly once from EVERY state (including `'closed'`) while the CLASS WRITE stays gated on the returned record's `changed`; (ii) `installSettingsModal`'s parameters are DROPPED (`(): void`) and its ONE call site is re-pointed in the same commit; (iii) the class mirror is driven by the adopted record's `changed` (the equivalence to the landed `settle` guard), with the RE-PARENT half KEPT** | 2026-09-28 (the `PD-UI-6` unit's landing pass; the unit contract is `docs/specs/unit-pd-ui-6-modal-state.md` `§2.1`/`§2.4`/`§2.6`/`§3.2` item 7, and the record of the gates is `archive/reviews/2026-09-28-PD-UI-6-doc-review.md`) | **What it pins.** **(i) THE ESCAPE ROUTE AND THE CLASS GATE — TWO DIFFERENT GATES, AND THAT IS THE DECISION.** The close route hands the adopted mechanism the verb **`'escape'`** (not `'close'`), so the callback is **INVOKED EXACTLY ONCE from EVERY one of the four states — including `'closed'`** — because *"a closed overlay that receives a dismissal is a live dismissal"*: this is the adopted obligation the fork's route must discharge, and it is **why the verb choice is a contract clause and not a style preference**. **WHILE the class write stays gated on the RETURNED RECORD'S `changed`** — so **an idempotent Escape performs NO class write**, and the fork's landed observable behaviour (the `settle` guard's no-write-on-no-op) is **PRESERVED, not replaced**. **The two gates are deliberately NOT the same gate, and the strongest false-green the unit's adversarial pass constructed lives exactly here:** *an implementation that **RECOMPUTES** the write guard from `isOpen()` instead of reading the adopted record's `changed` **passes every landed row*** — **the discriminating assertion is the WRITE COUNT, not the class**. That false-green is ruled out by the gate-4 amendment + TestWriter strengthening remand that **re-derived** `P-MD-TP-2`'s term (`18 → 22`, row `21 → 25`) and the declared total (`187 → 191`) and **proved discrimination** (`landed writes 0 / recomputing writes 1`), and it stays open on the **instrument** side only through the owed `X-2` doc-side derivation (`docs/pending.md`). **(ii) THE PARAMETERS ARE DROPPED.** `installSettingsModal` becomes **`(): void`** — the three optional parameters (`host`, `panels`, `mounts`) are **REMOVED from the pinned surface**, and the **ONE call site in `src/renderer/renderer.ts` is re-pointed in the same commit**. **What this CORRECTS is named:** `docs/specs/unit-u-shell-7-settings-modal.md` `§2.6`'s *"Signature (free shape, pinned inputs)"* clause — the landed implementation **read no member of any argument** (it resolved every element by literal id), so the three parameters carried a **DECLARED NO-REFERENT** (the `PD-UI-6` dossier's identifier row 8) and exactly one of two dispositions was owed to this spec. **No test may pass them; a pass that restores them re-opens the finding.** **(iii) THE CLASS MIRROR IS DRIVEN BY THE ADOPTED RECORD'S `changed`.** The mirror is the **consumer of the returned record** — `state` is observed and `changed` is the write guard — which is the **equivalence to the landed `settle` guard** this unit was required to demonstrate; the **adapter HOLDS the adopted state word** and `ModalVerb` is declared (exactly **ONE** vendored import: `src/shared/overlay.ts`). **THE RE-PARENT HALF IS KEPT:** the foundation **REFUSES** the move (`overlay.md` §3.4 `R-12`/`P-OV-10` — *"a RETURNED MOVE PLAN is a move with NO OBSERVABLE ON ANY LAYER THIS REPO OWNS"*), so the two mounts stay re-parented **fork-side** and **`DECIDED: MODAL-SETTINGS-REPARENT` STANDS UNCHANGED** — a refusal is recorded with its citation, **never absorbed as a silent divergence**. **THE SCOPE EXCLUSION BESIDE THESE CLAUSES, unchanged and NOT re-opened here:** the **applied inert-background write** is **out of this unit's scope** — `overlayInertDeclaration` is **adopted as DATA ONLY** and its applied half is the **minted, PARKED** row **`PD-OVERLAY-2`** (`DECIDED: POST-DIVISION-REBUILD-PD-UI-6-INERT-HALF-SCOPE-EXCLUSION`; `docs/specs/post-division-local-elimination-inventory.md` §2.1; `docs/pending.md`'s PARKED row). **Authority:** `../Provident-Electron/docs/specs/overlay.md` + `guide/overlay.md` (**read, never modified** — the vendored bytes are **digest-pinned: wrap, never patch**). | — the fork-side APPLIED inert-background write is NOT in `PD-UI-6`'s scope: the unit adopts the transition half and records `overlayInertDeclaration` as DATA; | **DECIDED: POST-DIVISION-REBUILD-PD-UI-1-ADAPTER-CONTRACTS — the fork's THEME ADAPTER has two clauses that are NOT derived from any earlier row: (1) honouring the vendored write record's `removal: true` means **THE ATTRIBUTE IS REMOVED** (not "perform no write"), and (2) the boot wiring's OS-listener liveness predicate is **SETTING-based** and discarding `applyThemeToRoot`'s return is **CORRECT** — the return itself stays required** | 2026-09-28 (the `PD-UI-1` gate-4 remand amendment, unit spec `§0B` items 2/3 — findings `A-7` and `A-11`; recorded by the unit's item-10d documentation review (`AGENTS.md` item 10d / `RCA-6`), pass record `archive/reviews/2026-09-28-PD-UI-1-doc-review.md`) | **(1) `A-7` — REMOVAL MEANS REMOVED; THE SKIP READING IS DIVERGENT.** A `ThemeAttributeWrite` whose `removal` member is `true` **names an attribute that is NOT PRESENT after the consumer applies the write**. The three outcomes the record can declare are its value arm, its removal arm and its echoed `name`; *"perform no write, leave a pre-existing attribute in place"* is a **THIRD outcome the record does not declare** and is **REJECTED**. **The ruling was made AGAINST the then-landed code** — `src/renderer/theme.ts` → `applyThemeToRoot` performed the write only when `removal` was false, leaving a pre-existing `data-theme` in place — **and that divergence has SINCE LANDED**: the adapter now performs `delete root.dataset.theme` on the removal arm, the mirror of `root.dataset.theme = write.value`, **inside the same `try` and at the SAME single write site** (so the `ThemeRoot` contract is **NOT** widened and no `removeAttribute` is taken). **The arm stays UNREACHABLE through the fork's own resolver** (`resolveTheme` returns `'light' | 'dark'`, always a non-empty string), so the difference is a **latent** divergence in a branch the resolver cannot enter — **not a repaired live defect, and NOT to be reported as one.** **Authority (read, never modified):** `../Provident-Electron/docs/guide/theme.md`'s applied-write recipe and UC-2 + `../Provident-Electron/docs/specs/theme.md` `§0A` note 2 / `§2.2` (D) / `§2.4` item 2 — the mechanism returns the write **as data**, the consumer performs it, and `removal` is the DECLARED discrimination. **No `docs/defects.md` → `docs/HANDOFF.md` row is owed** (`AGENTS.md` item 7 is for a package/foundation gap; this is **HOST-side**, and the foundation settles the record's half). **The tripwire that asserted the skip reading was REPLACED in the same landing** (`tests/pd-ui-1-theme-adoption.test.ts`'s `⟨A-7 RULED + A-2⟩ P-TH-TP-2` row now drives the RULING and requires a leave-in-place or `writes ''` corpus to FAIL) — **replaced, never deleted**: the row is the record that the ruling was made. **UNVERIFIED and parked to the live pass (`[U]`):** whether `dataset`-level deletion is the correct **mechanic** against a real `DOMStringMap` — the ruling fixes the record's **meaning**, not the mechanic. **Layer (RCA-12): `[T]` node/pure + an injected-record drive; NOT app-green — no applied attribute on a real `<html>` was observed.** **(2) `A-11` — THE LIVENESS PREDICATE STAYS SETTING-BASED; THE DISCARDED RETURN IS CORRECT.** The OS listener applies **only while the setting is neither `'light'` nor `'dark'`** — the **same strict-identity test against the same two literals** the kept precedence rule applies, so **the wiring holds no second precedence rule**; and **`installTheme`'s `apply()` discarding `applyThemeToRoot`'s return is CORRECT, not a defect**, because the return is **redundant by construction** with a datum the wiring already holds (the same `(setting, prefersDark())` pair) and a return-based predicate would be a **weaker** authority (it would silently invert if the resolution were ever computed differently). **`D-5` is UNMOVED:** `applyThemeToRoot` **still must return** the resolution it wrote — **the four exports and the return type are the kept surface**; what is ruled is only that **no `src/**` caller must consume it**. **This clause FALSIFIES `docs/specs/unit-pd-ui-1-theme.md` `§2.2` item 4 AS FILED** (*"the `applyThemeToRoot` return feeds it"*), which is amended **annotate-beside** there; **and it makes `src/renderer/theme.ts` → `applyThemeToRoot`'s doc comment** (*"Returns the resolved theme so the caller can decide whether the OS listener is live"*) **a STALE CLAIM owed a CORRECTED form** — **OWNED to the next `PD-UI-1` landing pass (Implementer) and still uncorrected at this head** (the fix is a correction, not a deletion: the return stays; only the liveness claim goes). **Layer (RCA-12): `[H]` host-side source-text; NOT app-green — no OS flip was observed.** **WHAT THIS ROW DOES NOT DECIDE:** `A-8`/`D-13` (no register row is generator-backed; the `≤100`/`≤120`/`STOP AFTER 5` machinery is DECLARED INAPPLICABLE to this register) gets **no** row — it governs **this unit's own register execution design**, changes no caller-visible or shared contract, and is fully carried by the unit spec's `§4`/`§0B` item 1/`D-13`; and the **`E-5` cap overshoot (`194 > 120`) and the `E-8` mocked-record mechanism question remain OPEN ARCHITECT items** (not resolved here). | unit spec `docs/specs/unit-pd-ui-1-theme.md` `§0B` items 2/3, `§2.1` items 3/6, `§2.2` item 4, `§3.2` item 5, `§8` `D-12`/`D-14`, `§9` item 1(e)/item 10; blind-greens artifact `docs/specs/unit-pd-ui-1-theme-greens.md` (`X-1`, rows `B2`/`B4`/`B5`/`B7`, `F3`, `G4`); `src/renderer/theme.ts` (`applyThemeToRoot`), `src/renderer/renderer.ts` (`installTheme`); `../Provident-Electron/docs/guide/theme.md`; `../Provident-Electron/docs/specs/theme.md` `§0A` note 2 / `§2.2` (D) / `§2.4` item 2 |

| **DECIDED: PROVIDENT-FOCUS-ADOPTS-THE-FOUNDATION-CONTRACT — `provident.focus`'s arguments become `{ target?: string, newTab?: boolean }`: `tabId` is DROPPED and `target` is re-typed from the five-kind object union to a string. | **DECIDED: PAGE-COMMIT-CONTRACT — ONE `applyBatch` per page commit, and success requires an EXACTLY-acknowledged `BatchResult` (one agreeing result per op)** | 2026-09-22 (`C9 U-EDIT-1` landing; the contract is the unit spec's §3.3/§3.6 + the §11.11 item 1 correction of §11.10 item 4(d)) | **What it pins.** **(1) ONE CALL.** One commit = one `IPC_EDIT_BATCH` payload (`EditBatchPayload { ops: BatchOp[] }`) → `handleEditBatch` → **one** `store.applyBatch(ops)`. Not a loop, not `store.enqueue`, not per-node writes; the channel is existing (**no new IPC channel**); the cache is not written and the store is not optimistically touched. **(2) ONE JOURNAL ENTRY.** A successful batch lands as exactly one invertible `batch` journal entry (reverse-ordered inverse); **COARSE undo** (one commit = one undo step) is an explicit decision — no finer granularity without a supersession. **(3) ONE PERSIST.** The store's own invariant is inherited (`applyBatchSync`: 1 `persist()` on success, 0 on failure/empty); the commit adds no second persistence path. **(4) THE RESULT IS READ — AND THE ACKNOWLEDGEMENT IS EXACT.** The landed reader is **stricter** than the first reading: `isAcknowledgedBatch` requires a boolean `ok: true`, a `results` array whose length **equals** the op count (**an over-long array is no longer success**), and **every** entry agreeing with its op through `acknowledgesOp`/`acknowledgedRecordId` (the entry's `op` kind must match, and any identity it carries must be the acknowledged op's own; an absent identity claims nothing). An absent/partial/junk/mis-acknowledging result is the typed **`store-rejected`** failure (`storeRejectedFailure`) carrying the record's `error`/`failedIndex` **verbatim** and fabricating neither, with the dirty flag **kept** and **no auto-retry**; a commit that reports success without an acknowledged write is `FS19` — "the worst outcome in this unit". **(5) FAILURE LEAVES THE STORE UNCHANGED** (bytes equal, 0 persists, journal delta 0) and sets the **host-side** per-tab `commit-failed` state. (The unit's §11.10 item 4(d) reading — "at least as long as the op count" — is **SUPERSEDED by this row's clause (4)**; both clauses are recorded at the spec, the superseded one marked in place.) | `docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §3.3 items 1–6/§3.5/§3.6/§11.8 item 3/§11.10 item 4(d) (SUPERSEDED)/§11.11 item 1; `DECIDED: BATCH-ATOMICITY-API`; `docs/specs/unit-import-batch-persist.md` §2a/§2b  **Tests that pin it:** `src/renderer/sidebar-panes.ts` `isAcknowledgedBatch`/`acknowledgesOp`/`acknowledgedRecordId`/`storeRejectedFailure` read against `src/main/rag-store.ts` `BatchResult`; `tests/page-commit-failure-visibility.test.ts` `C1` 282 / `C2` 311 / `C5` 334 / `T1` 499 / `T2` 514 (ZERO `edit.batch` for an unchanged page, exactly ONE for one change) / `C3` 357 (no fabricated `failedIndex`) · `tests/page-commit-scope-ack-race.test.ts` `N3` 425 + control 462 · `tests/page-diff.test.ts` 857 (one `applyBatch`, never a per-op loop) · `tests/unit-u-edit-1-property-register.test.ts` P-IM-2 844 |
| **DECIDED: PAGE-STATE-OWNED-BY-THE-TAB — the page-edit subject is the ACTIVE TAB id; the dirty set, the failure map and the caret store are per-tab, and a document id is NEVER a subject** | 2026-09-22 (the `U-STAGE-ACTIVE-TAB` landing + the `C9 U-EDIT-1` landing's §11.8 item 3 carrier) | **What it pins.** **(1) THE SUBJECT.** `SidebarPanes.pageEditSurfaceHandlerSubject()` returns `this.activeTabId ?? PAGE_EDIT_SURFACE_ID` — **never a document id** (`FS-4`); the fallback applies **iff** there is no active tab. **(2) THE STATE.** The dirty set (`Set<string>`), the commit-failure map (`Map<tabId, failure>`) and the caret store (`Map<string, CaretState>`) are keyed by that subject; **two tabs on the SAME document never share any of the three** (`FS-5`) — typing in one marks only it dirty, a failure attaches only to it, and its caret restores only for it. Only the key's **value** changed; the maps' types did not. **(3) CLOSE DROPS EXACTLY THE CLOSED SUBJECTS.** `dropClosedPageState` drops the closed tab's dirty flag, failure record **and its page caret** (the strip's id allocator re-mints a closed LAST tab's id, so a surviving caret under that id would restore against the NEW tab's document) — never another tab's. **(4) THE CARET'S DOCUMENT CHECK IS A SEPARATE CARRIER.** `EditControllerOptions.isDocumentLive` (+ the non-enumerable `setDocumentLiveness`) owns liveness; the **host-supplied** `pageSubjectDocument` hook owns the subject→document mapping, and the caret is read **FIRST** (a caret-less read returns `undefined` WITHOUT consulting the hook — the §A.1.4 pinned order); `doc === null` or a not-live document **clears** the entry. `backRefs` keeps its documented `Map<ragNodeId, nodeId[]>` invariant — **`seedActiveDocumentRef()` is DELETED**, so no document id is ever a `backRefs` key. The absent-hook path stays byte-for-byte legacy (`backRefs.has(subjectId)`). **(5) THE WARNING IS HOST STATE.** The `commit-failed` record lives in the host map and is authored into the graph as provident data (`pane-graph.ts` `PAGE_COMMIT_WARNING_ID`/`pageCommitWarningContent`) — never a DOM class/attribute — so it survives a re-derive by construction and is cleared on a later success. | `docs/specs/unit-stage-active-tab-display.md` §5.1 I1, §5.3.2, §5.3.5 item 3, §A.1.4, §A.3.2; `docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §3.5 items 3/4/6, §3.6, §11.8 item 3, §11.11 item 1; `DECIDED: WHOLE-PAGE-EDITING` **THE TESTS THAT PIN IT:** `tests/page-commit-tab-ownership.test.ts` `T1` 391 / `T2` 410 / `T3` 423 / `T4` 444 / `T5` 486 / `T6` 546 / `T7` 579 · `tests/page-commit-failure-visibility.test.ts` `W1` 423 / `W2` 448 / `W3` 476 (post-fix re-run OWED) · `tests/unit-stage-active-tab-display.test.ts` C1–C9, `S12` 843, `P-TP-2` 869 · `tests/unit-stage-active-tab-display-contract-holes.test.ts` `H-5` 598 · `tests/unit-stage-active-tab-display-blind-contradictions.test.ts` `S5`–`S13` |
| **DECIDED: STAGE-OWNERSHIP-PREDICATE-AND-THE-PRE-TAB-CARVE-OUT — the stage's owning document is `stageOwner(s)`, the SURFACE scope is a predicate distinct from the BODY scope, and the pre-tab boot state is an explicit carve-out** | 2026-09-22 (`U-STAGE-ACTIVE-TAB`, §A.3.1 ruling C1; the readers landed the same day) | **What it pins.** **(1) THE PREDICATE (`stageOwner`, total).** `('tab-document', getActiveDocumentId(s))` when an active tab exists and its kind is `document`; **else** `('pre-tab', getPreTabDocumentId(s))` iff `isPreTabState(s)` and that id is non-null; **else** `('none', null)`. **(2) THE SURFACE CENSUS.** The live `#page-edit-surface` count in the stage region is **`1` iff `stageOwner(s).kind ∈ {'tab-document','pre-tab'}`, else `0`**; when it is `1`, that root's `data-edit-surface` **equals** `stageOwner(s).id`. **Both discriminators** (the authored id and the marker) are counted and must **agree**; there is no duplicate and no stale root (`Runtime.applyContentReconcile`'s `destroyRoot` admits the same closed root set `extractContentRoots` admits, and a root `next` still authors is **never** destroyed). **(3) THE PRE-TAB CARVE-OUT.** `isPreTabState()` is **monotonic**: `true` iff no `mountTab`/`mountTabs` has been handed; the pre-tab stage authors **exactly one** surface for the boot seam's retained default-context document (`_currentDocumentId`, else the alphabetically-first doc-head). **The strict arm binds from the first tab state onward** — `mountTab(null)`, every non-document kind and `mountTabs` with no active entry stay **`0`** (and a stale pre-tab root must be destroyed there). **It is NOT a clause that the pre-tab document equals the first tab's document** (measured: with `defaultDocumentId='doc-b'` persisted the pre-tab surface still carries `doc-a`); the first `mountTab` re-owns. **(4) THE TWO SCOPES ARE DISTINCT.** `stageDocumentScope()` keeps its pinned **BODY/document** semantics (no tab ⇒ the retained selection) while the **SURFACE** is authored from `stageOwner(s)` alone (`stageOwnerDocumentScope()`); the two are separate predicates, and this split is what makes H-3's `mountTabs` arm strict. **(5) WHAT IT DOES NOT LICENSE.** No `_currentDocumentId` read at a stage-mount/re-derive/**page-subject** seam — `FS-4`/`FS-2`/`FS-3` respectively. | `docs/specs/unit-stage-active-tab-display.md` §A.3.1 clauses (a)–(g), §A.3.2, §5.1 I2 clause 3 (SUPERSEDED IN PART at the boot arm), §5.3.5 rule 1, §A.1.1/§A.1.2; the `U-EDIT-1` "FOCUSED" reading is `docs/specs/unit-stage-active-tab-display.md` §5.3.5 (the item-10d note owed to `docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §2.1/§11.7)  **Tests that pin it:** `tests/unit-stage-active-tab-display-blind-contradictions.test.ts` `S1` 411 / `S2` 462 · `tests/unit-stage-active-tab-display-contract-holes.test.ts` `H-3` 474 · `tests/unit-stage-active-tab-display-pbt-generators.test.ts` `P-SM-1`'s boot draw · the **seven** committed boot rows §A.3.1 lists as untouched (§A.3.3's blast radius) · `tests/unit-stage-active-tab-display.test.ts` `R8` 1089 (the `destroyRoot` widening, both arms) |
| **DECIDED: REPRESENTATION-MODE-SUCCESSOR — the removed `editingMode` control is replaced by `representationMode: 'html' \| 'markdown'` on the operator-settings carrier; the mode-change BROADCAST contract survives** | 2026-09-22 (`C9 U-EDIT-1` landing; `docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §2.3/§2.5/§11.8 item 2) | **What it pins.** The representation mode is carried by the existing persisted operator state (`DECIDED: UI-CONFIG-CARRIER`) as the **new field `representationMode`** with the two-member union `'html' | 'markdown'` (`RepresentationMode = 'html' | 'markdown'`; `representationMode?: RepresentationMode` on both `OperatorSettings` and `OperatorSettingsPatch`), coerced by `src/main/operator-settings-store.ts` `coerceRepresentationMode` with an **`'html'` default**; a patch **without** the field leaves the stored representation unchanged. The removed `editingMode` union is **NOT reused** (its members named editing *controls*; the successor names *representations* of one control — reusing the token is the drift `ST-6` closes), and a stored legacy `editingMode` from an earlier session is **ignored, never trusted** (`FS20`). **The surviving clause of `EDITING-MODE-SETTING` that MUST be kept:** a mode change writes the operator store → main broadcasts `operator-settings-changed` with **the store's result as the authoritative payload** → the host uses the payload directly (never a re-fetch) → a **fresh re-derive** (`requestRebuild` → `reDerive`, never `refresh()` over a cached spliced envelope). **Not app-green:** the live battery records that the toggle flips `data-mode` + the toolbar label while the **stage keeps rich HTML** (`docs/defects.md` `U-EDIT-1-LIVE-4`) — the contract here is the *carrier*, not the rendered result. | `docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §2.3/§2.5/§5 item 5/§11.8 item 2/§11.9; `docs/specs/live-battery-2026-09-22-page-commit-and-stage.md` §3.2 (`U-EDIT-1-LIVE-4`)  **Tests that pin it:** `src/main/operator-settings-store.ts` `coerceRepresentationMode`; `tests/representation-mode.test.ts`; `tests/unit-u-edit-1-greens.md` `F1`/`F2`/`F3` (blind); `tests/single-editable-surface.test.ts` `FS21` rows 643/652/661/675 (no authored textarea / zero rendered `<textarea>`) |
| **DECIDED: REBUILD-ARCHIVE-POLICY — a test whose subject the specs supersede is ARCHIVED, not adapted; the archive is the REBUILD's INPUT; the coverage hole is RECORDED, not hidden; and no archived file may be restored except through its owning unit's gate cycle** | 2026-09-21 (product-owner ruling: **this is a rebuild pass to correct drift and enforce feature compliance with the specs; it is a BREAKING change; the changed/obsoleted features' tests are archived NOW so they can be rebuilt in proper gated passes later**) | **The rule (governs every future test retirement; SUPERSEDES `DECIDED: PRUNING-FOLLOWS-THE-IMPLEMENTING-UNIT`).** **(1) ARCHIVED, NOT ADAPTED.** When the specs supersede a test's subject, the file is **moved out of `tests/**` into `archive/tests/<date>-<name>.test.ts`** — it is **never** edited, relaxed, skipped, `todo`'d or rewritten in place to keep the suite green. Adapting a pin to a superseded contract hides the drift; the archive makes it visible. **(2) THE ARCHIVE IS THE REBUILD'S INPUT.** The behaviours an archived file pinned are **re-derived from the SPEC** by the **owning unit's own gated pass** — spec → TestWriter red (reported) → Implementer green → RCA-3 adversarial/PBT → item-10d doc review → DONE row naming the layer — with a **FRESH red set**, derived from `docs/specs/design-extensions-review.md` §13 + the unit's own spec + the affected `docs/decisions.md` rows. The archived file is consulted as an input to **what the old pin covered**, never as the source of the new contract, and its assertions are never copied back. **(3) THE COVERAGE HOLE IS RECORDED, NOT HIDDEN.** Every retirement records its **file count / row count / skip count before → after** as a READING from `npm test` (never a prediction), the **owning unit** and the **spec the rebuilt suite derives from** (`docs/specs/test-pruning-disposition-2026-09-21.md` §9's rebuild map), and the resulting **citation debt** (`docs/pending.md` + that audit's §9.7; `tests/**` citations are repointed by the owning unit, since a citation pointing at a moved file is a gate violation — AGENTS.md item 6c). **(4) NO RESTORE EXCEPT THROUGH THE CYCLE.** No archived file may be moved back into `tests/**` as a shortcut: a restored pin re-enters through its owning unit's cycle with its own red set, because restoring the file would re-pin superseded behaviour. **(5) THE FENCES STILL HOLD.** Nothing in this policy authorises touching `tests/fixtures/**` (shared with KEEP-LIVE tests), the two §14.2 fence files (`tests/traversal.test.ts`, `tests/import-render-no-duplicates.test.ts`), the O-0 oracle suites/artifact pair (§13.3), the `vitest.config.ts` 15 000 ms budget tenant (`tests/unit-v5-migration-contract.test.ts`) or the `.test.mjs` battery leg. **(6) A TEST MAY NEVER BE DELETED BY A PASS OF THIS CLASS:** moves only (`git mv`), bytes preserved and `md5sum`-verified, with the count reported. | product-owner ruling 2026-09-21 (the archive-rebuild pass); execution record + per-file rebuild map + coverage delta: `docs/specs/test-pruning-disposition-2026-09-21.md` §9 (§9.3 moves/exclusions/restores, §9.4 rebuild map, §9.5 coverage delta, §9.6 the red and its cause, §9.7 citation debt); topic: `archive/README.md` (`tests/`, *"rebuild inputs, not discards"*); superseded predecessor: the row above |
| **DECIDED: FENCE-REPLAN-U-EDIT-1-CHILD-LIST-ROW — ONE fence row in `tests/traversal.test.ts` (the authored-child-list assertion, the `textarea-<ragId>` entry) is RE-PLANNED under an explicit owner ruling; the authorization is scoped to that file and that row and licenses nothing else** | 2026-09-21 (product-owner ruling, recorded at `docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §5.1 + §6.5 + §11 amendment `11.9` item 2) | **The rule.** The two fence suites (`tests/traversal.test.ts`, `tests/import-render-no-duplicates.test.ts`) are **EXEMPT BY NAME**: their standing pin is *"must stay green UNCHANGED"* / *"may not be re-derived"* (`docs/specs/design-extensions-review.md` §3.1/§3.2 B.4, §14.2), and both sit on the archived-test pass's **absolute-exclusion** list (`docs/specs/test-pruning-disposition-2026-09-21.md` §9.3a). The fence therefore **cannot be edited by a unit on its own authority** — a fence change is a **gate decision**, and this row records the one such decision taken. **The ruling:** `ST-6`/`PRUNE-311` require textarea editing to be **removed** ("no `<textarea>` editors are materialized"), while `tests/traversal.test.ts` pinned the traversal-authored `textarea-ul` child in its child-list assertion — so the owner **authorized re-planning that one row**. **The re-plan shape (pinned).** The row asserts the authored child list **without the `textarea-<ragId>` entry** (`[undefined, 'rag-li1', …]` for the `ul` subtree root), because `src/main/traversal.ts` `buildSubtree` now authors **no** `textarea` child at all; the row's **subject** (the child ordering at that position) is unchanged. **The scope, stated so it cannot be widened:** the authorization covers **`tests/traversal.test.ts`'s child-list row ONLY** — every other pin in that file and **every** assertion in `tests/import-render-no-duplicates.test.ts` stay untouched, and a pass that rewrites another fence assertion, edits the other fence file or re-derives a different row is a **review finding**. The fence stays a **green control** in every red run outside the one authorized row. **Why it is not a licence:** the exemption exists so a unit cannot quietly adapt a fence to its own convenience; this row is a **recorded, owner-made exception with the subject held fixed**, which is the only legal shape for a fence change. | product-owner ruling 2026-09-21, via `docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §5.1/§6.5/§11.9 item 2 |

it stands as the ruling record.** | product-owner ruling 2026-09-21 (`docs/specs/unit-u-edit-1-whole-page-editing.md` §4.1/§4.2/§11.9 item 1) |
| **DECIDED: VITEST-5-ELECTRON-44-TOOLCHAIN-MIGRATION (DEC-2) — the vitest-5 / electron-44 / esbuild test-harness + config migration; the trio's `npm test` leg is restored by a re-derived mock-capture pattern + a committed test-timeout budget** | 2026-09-20 (ruling) · **LANDED 2026-09-21** (spec `docs/specs/unit-v5-migration.md`; defect row `docs/defects.md` `SUITE-RED-AFTER-VITEST5-ELECTRON44` = **FIXED**) | **The row this id NAMES now exists (minted by the handover-staleness audit 2026-09-21), because the tracker cited it and it had none** — the catalog's `PRUNE-393` cites `DECIDED: DEC-2 VITEST-5 / ELECTRON-44 TOOLCHAIN MIGRATION`, a title that did not resolve in this file (the catalog's own pointer discipline requires it to). **What it pins:** the devDependency bump (`electron ^33.4.11 → ^44.4.3`, `esbuild ^0.24.0 → ^0.28.2`, `vitest ^2.0.0 → ^5.0.1`) left the suite RED for **22 tests in 7 files** (drifting to 25 in 8 files); the migration is **its own unit** (spec → reported red → green → adversarial → doc review → DONE row) that (a) **re-derives the electron-mock capture pattern** under vitest-5's `clearMocks: true` default (the `capturedBridge()`-undefined class: mock `calls[]` history recorded at MODULE EVALUATION is cleared between collection and each test — fix the pattern, never the default), and (b) commits an explicit **`testTimeout: 15_000`** budget in `vitest.config.ts` for the depth-10 000 totality rows (5.4–5.8 s under load vs vitest 5's 5 000 ms default). **A `clearMocks: false` override, or any per-run flag, is NOT the fix** — it re-freezes old semantics and hides the class. **Boundary this row does not lift:** the migration verifies the **test-harness/config layer only** (`src/**` behavior change = 0), so its green is a harness green, never app-green (RCA-12). | user **DEC-2**, 2026-09-20; `docs/pending.md`'s SCHEDULED row (now LANDED); `docs/specs/unit-v5-migration.md` |
| **DECIDED: PRUNING-FOLLOWS-THE-IMPLEMENTING-UNIT — a decision-level obsolescence does NOT obsolete a test; the code change lands FIRST and the test archive follows the removal IN THE SAME unit with its red set re-derived** | **SUPERSEDED 2026-09-21 by `DECIDED: REBUILD-ARCHIVE-POLICY`** (read that row for the governing rule; this row is retained as provenance only, and its Clauses 2-4 are NO LONGER the rule) · original date 2026-09-21 (the `R-2` ruling adopting `docs/specs/test-pruning-disposition-2026-09-21.md` §6) | **The rule.** A recorded supersession (`DECIDED: ENGINE-AUTHORITATIVE-DOCUMENT-CRUD` / `GN-1`, `DECIDED: ENGINE-ABSENT-REFUSES-WITHOUT-AN-ENGINE` / `GN-4`, the `ST-6` textarea removal, the catalog-generator park) is **CONTRACT-LEVEL ONLY** and does **NOT** obsolete any test by itself — a supersession is a destination, not a removal (gate record `docs/specs/design-extensions-review.md` §13.2 **S1**: *"the contract says engine-authoritative while the code still writes locally; that gap is RECORDED … never hidden"*). **Clause 1 — the order is fixed:** the CODE CHANGE comes first, and the test archive follows the removal **inside the SAME unit's cycle** (spec re-derivation → reported TestWriter red run → Implementer green → adversarial → blind greens → doc review → DONE row), with the unit's **red set re-derived**, never inherited (§13.3: *"one unit = one spec, one red run, one green"*). **Clause 2 — the ONE out-of-cycle ground:** a test may be archived outside a unit's cycle **ONLY** when its subject is **already absent from `src/**`** — module absent, symbol absent, or the calling branch unreachable (the audit's §2.8 `ARCHIVE-READY` criteria) — the `ARCHIVE-READY` class, which is **EMPTY today (0 of 224 files)**, so **no test may be archived today at all**. A decision text is **never** such a ground. **Clause 3 — the 26 `ORPHANED-PIN` repoints (2026-09-21, executed) are CITATION HYGIENE, NOT PRUNING:** their subjects are live, so repointing moved no file, retired no pin and changed no count (audit §4.2/§6.1 item 4: *"`ARCHIVE` is not the first move for an orphaned citation — `REPOINT` is"*). **Clause 4 — the recorded free-up ORDER (the `HOLD-UNTIL-IMPLEMENTED` set, 44 files):** **C9 `U-EDIT-1`** (`WHOLE-PAGE-EDITING`: `ST-1` non-live-markdown half, `ST-3`, `ST-4`, `ST-5`, `ST-6`) frees **19** (§3.3.1; it also closes the defect `EDIT-MODE-TEXTAREA-UI`) → **C11 `U-SEARCH`** (the `C13`-removal half of `PN-1`) frees **1** (§3.3.2) → **P2 `U-AUTHORITY-SWITCH` (`O-8`)** frees **17** (§3.3.3) → **P2 `U-READS-PIVOT`** frees **7** (§3.3.4) — all sitting **behind the `U-ENGINE-PERSIST` + `U-CORPUS-MIGRATION` prerequisites** (§6.2 item 5: *"without them the corpus is lost at restart, so no cutover-driven archive may precede them"*; `O0_OPERATOR_DOCUMENTS = 226` must not be re-seeded). **Clause 5 — no move without the trio reading recorded** (§5.5), no move across a contract regime (§13.2 `S2`), and no move touching the fence pair (`tests/traversal.test.ts` + `tests/import-render-no-duplicates.test.ts`) or the O-0 oracle pair (§13.3); a test-archive topic would be a **new** `archive/tests/` row created ONLY by the first executed move (`archive/README.md`). | `docs/specs/test-pruning-disposition-2026-09-21.md` §6 (the order-of-operations rule + §6.1/§6.2/§6.3), §3.0/§3.1 (the `ARCHIVE-READY` = 0 evidence), §3.3 (the 44-file `HOLD` blocks), §4.2/§4.3 and §5.2/§5.5; the ruling is that audit's §7 **R-2** (*"adopt §6 as the program's order-of-operations rule and record it as a decision row"*), and this row is the adoption; **precedent for why the rule is binding:** the 2026-08-26 sweep moved docs without checking the test side, leaving the 26-file citation debt recorded as the defect `TEST-CITES-MISSING-SPEC` in `docs/defects.md` |
| **DECIDED: O0-SNAPSHOT-CLONE-STRUCTURAL-ACCEPTED — O-0's artifact is accepted as the ARCH-GNOSIS-OFFLOAD gate input in the OPEN-structural form; an accepted-structural report opens the O-5 gate** | 2026-09-20 | **The acceptance.** O-0's committed artifact (`docs/specs/unit-o-0-per-stage-breakdown.md`, third edition, host clock 2026-09-20; spec `docs/specs/unit-o-0-per-stage-measurement.md` §12.13) is ACCEPTED as the ARCH-GNOSIS-OFFLOAD gate input **in the `status:"OPEN-structural"` form**. **(1) The accepted structural gap — `snapshot.clone` (verbatim reason).** No channel carries the main recorder's records into the renderer/report (the MAIN instance IS armed via `ASTROGRAPHER_O0_MAIN_ARM=1` and wraps the `IPC_RAG_SNAPSHOT` handler, but `driver.mainSeamArmed:false`, `mainTransport.channel:null` and no `instance:"main"` record exists in any `hook.stageRecords`) **AND** Electron's structured-clone serialization happens inside the IPC internals AFTER the handler returns, outside every host-side wrap — so the main-side handler share and the serialization share are both UNMEASURED. **(2) The derived `post.style` residual is recorded as UNCOMPUTABLE** (`source:"derived"`, `ms:null`, `unseparated:true`, negative on every row: −97.5 folder / −1 885 document) — no style/layout figure is offered and none may be quoted. **(3) The AMENDED O-5 gate condition (the spec is the canonical home — `unit-o-0-per-stage-measurement.md` §3.6b RUL-4 clause 7, amended):** the O-5 delegation gate opens at `status:"OK"` ⇔ `pass:true` **OR** at an `OPEN-structural` report whose ONLY gap is the ACCEPTED structural set (`snapshot.clone` + the therefore-derived `post.style`) **provided** `selfValidation.ok:true`, `gatingReasons:[]`, the window-bound check PASSES on every row, and the structural reasons are recorded VERBATIM (plus the mandatory `reconciliation.note`). Any other structural id, an imputation, a falsifiability failure, a missing reason/note or any gating reason does NOT open the gate. **SUPERSEDED CLAUSE (recorded here; the spec clause is the canonical home):** the old "ONLY `status:"OK"` ⇔ `pass:true` opens the O-5 gate" (RUL-4 clause 7 pre-amendment; also restated in `docs/next-steps.md` and the artifact's §14). **(4) The visibility invariant.** The absence of a main-side transport must stay VISIBLE in every report: O-0's reports read `status:"OPEN-structural"` and are NEVER quietly re-labelled `"OK"`. **(5) A future main-side transport is a SEPARATE unit** (an engine/host seam carrying the main recorder's records; its own gate, its own red set per RCA-2, never a patch inside O-0) — it is NOT a prerequisite of the O-5 gate any more. **(6) The measured O-0 inputs this row pins (third run, GPU-OFF, folder-row vs document-open ms):** `traversal.build` **684.2 / 14.7**; `reconcile.apply` **150.9 / 2085.1**; `render.ssr` **76.1 / 1742.5**; JS path **87.25 % / 94.76 %**; A-4 read counts **1 / 2** (`IPC_RAG_SNAPSHOT`); long-task totals **990 / 2 222 ms** (GPU-ON 1 032 / 2 213); census **226/226 documents** (6 102 nodes / 9 266 edges, recorded provenance); `env.engine:"absent"` with the `fetch failed` evidence. **The O-4 trigger: the traversal WALK is load-bearing for the folder-row DISCLOSURE (69.11 % GPU-OFF / 69.34 % GPU-ON) and NOT for the document open** (`reconcile.apply` 93.84 %, `render.ssr` second at 1 742.5 ms) → parked trigger (c) stays **CANDIDATE-FIRED for the disclosure only**. **(7) What the acceptance does NOT close (OWED):** `O0-VALIDATOR-DROPS-POSTDERIVATION-REASONS` and `O0-MEASUREMENT-SHAPE-DOUBLE-REDERIVE` stay OPEN in `docs/defects.md` — the user accepted the STRUCTURAL gap, not these two. | user decision 2026-09-20 (DEC-1) closing the O-0 pass; spec `docs/specs/unit-o-0-per-stage-measurement.md` §3.6b clause 7 / §11 / §12.13; artifact `docs/specs/unit-o-0-per-stage-breakdown.md` |

| **DECIDED: PANE-DRAG-HEADER-ONLY + DEFERRED-POINTER-CAPTURE — the pane-drag gesture surface is the pane HEADER (never the pane body), and the gesture claims pointer capture only after a real drag threshold** | 2026-09-15 | A `pointerdown` inside a `.pane-frame[data-pane-id]` starts a pane relocation ONLY from the pane header region (`GESTURE_SELECTOR = '.gutter[data-zone], .pane-collapse-toggle'`; the frame is resolved from the header for the pane id + the capture target); a pointerdown on pane BODY content (any provident-compiled element carrying `data-node-id`) never starts a drag and never requests pointer capture, so the body control's own `click` reaches its handler. **Clause 2 (F-1b, found during the live fix):** the gesture does NOT call `setPointerCapture` at the pointerdown — an immediate capture on the frame retargets the gesture's OWN `click` to the capturing frame, which made the pane HEADER's collapse/expand toggle inert too. Capture is claimed lazily in `onGestureMove` once travel ≥ `PANE_DRAG_CAPTURE_THRESHOLD` (4px): a pure click never captures and always reaches its target; a real drag still captures, so the move/up routing is unchanged. Rationale: the frame-wide surface + immediate capture made every clickable row inside an app-graph pane — and the header toggle — inert to a real user (defect **F-1**, live-confirmed and then FIXED + LIVE-CONFIRMED 2026-09-15). | defect F-1 (`docs/defects.md`, `docs/specs/user-flow-audit-coverage-2026-09-15.md`); pinned by `tests/renderer-pane-drag-surface.test.ts` + the re-pinned `tests/unit-u-shell-shell-wiring{,-adversarial}.test.ts` |

| **DECIDED: PLACEMENT-ONLY-PAYLOAD-ROOT — only a payload root carries the zone `targetPlacement`; nested subtrees are reached through their owner's route** | 2026-09-16 | In `buildTraversal`/`buildSubtree` (`src/main/traversal.ts`) the zone `placement: { targetPlacement: [zoneName] }` is authored ONLY on a subtree emitted as its OWN payload root (a section, or a genuine multi-parent duplicate). A nested doc-child subtree carries NO placement anchor: the placement-path enumeration mints ONE route per anchor, so a second anchor on a nested node produced a second route for that node and the render emitted it TWICE (nested inside its section AND again as a zone-level sibling) — the user-visible duplicate-element defect (LIVE-UF6 / report #1 I-3/I-4). The line-map measurement (`renderSubtreeMarkdown`) re-adds the anchor on a CLONE so the markdown ranges are unchanged.
