| **DECIDED: STORE-ID-PREFIX — non-default-store imports mint `<name>:`-prefixed documentIds at the import minting seam; the default store's output is byte-equal to today; the prefix namespace is reserved (A1)** | 2026-09-05 | `importMarkdownCorpus` prefixes the documentId passed to `parseMarkdown` with `<name>:` for NON-default stores (`markdown-import.ts:249-286`); the default store stays unprefixed (byte-equal). Safe because `sanitizeDocumentId` strips `:` (`markdown-import.ts:69-76`) and the registry name charset excludes it — no default-store documentId can contain `:`. A1 (binding): the prefix namespace is reserved at the NODE-id level too — a default-store document whose sanitized basename EQUALS a registered non-default store name mints prefix-shaped node ids (`markdown-parse.ts:444,565` — `` `${documentId}:${type}:${n}` ``); the spec pins one of (a) the default store's import seam rejects such a documentId, or (b) id-based resolution derives the store from a recognized prefix and fails loud on ambiguity. A foreign-store id passed to an id-based tool without `store` MISSES in the default store (the existing `node not found` fail-state) instead of silently mutating a same-named node (closes the critique's B3 mutation hazard). `edit.create_node` mints `n-${randomUUID()}` — already globally unique. **LANDED + A1-RESOLUTION-(a) (2026-09-05, Unit U-MS4 — spec `docs/specs/unit-ms4-id-prefixing.md`, greens 41/0/2, `tests/unit-ms4-id-prefixing.test.ts` 40/40 + `unit-ms4-id-prefixing-adversarial.test.ts` 9/9):** the `<name>:` prefix mint (§5.2 step 4g, `markdown-import.ts:279`) + the A1 default-seam REJECTION (resolution (a), §5.4 — the byte-pinned `documentId collides with a registered store name` message, A1-S7 erratum `includes(base)` take-as-given) + the SC1–SC5 invalid-store-context battery + the foreign-id-miss guarantee (INV-4) are all LANDED.** | `docs/specs/multi-document-store-config-review.md` §2 D4 + §8 A1 (four-agent gate, user go-ahead 2026-09-05) |
| **DECIDED: IMPORT-ROOT-PER-STORE — `edit.import_markdown` uses the ADDRESSED store's `corpusRoot` as the containment root; relative `files` resolve against it; the tool schema stays files-only (ADV-1); the description is corrected** | 2026-09-05 | The `edit.import_markdown` tool uses the addressed store's configured `corpusRoot` as the containment root; relative `files` paths resolve against the STORE's root (`resolve(corpusRoot, file)` — `markdown-import.ts:203` changes from `resolve(file)`; byte-identical for the default store when `corpusRoot === process.cwd()`, A5). The TOOL schema stays `files`-only (ADV-1 preserved, `unit-t-markdown-import.md:183`) — the seam is fed server-side from the registry, never by the caller, and the `store` argument cannot influence any path (adversarial-pinned). The tool description at `mcp-server.ts:1078` is CORRECTED in the same unit ("fixed server-side per store: the addressed store's configured corpus root; the default store's root is its configured corpus root (the project root when unconfigured)"). **Amended 2026-09-05 (F-MS2-6, the U-MS2 adversarial batch):** the row originally quoted the pre-amendment tail — "the default store's root is the project root" — which was factually wrong for a default entry with a CONFIGURED `corpusRoot`; the byte-pinned description string was amended to "…the default store's root is its configured corpus root (the project root when unconfigured)" (the rest byte-identical; spec `docs/specs/unit-ms2-store-wiring.md` §5.2's F-MS2-6 amendment — the code string and the sanctioned test-20 re-pin carry the amended wording). ONE-WAY-SNAPSHOT holds per store (each store's import is its own one-shot batch journal entry). The realpath/TOCTOU discipline (`markdown-import.ts:207-248`) is reused UNCHANGED per store root. **LANDED importer half (2026-09-05, Unit U-MS4 — spec `docs/specs/unit-ms4-id-prefixing.md`, greens 41/0/2, `tests/unit-ms4-id-prefixing.test.ts` 40/40 + `unit-ms4-id-prefixing-adversarial.test.ts` 9/9):** the importer's internal re-point (`markdown-import.ts:203`) + the per-store containment/TOCTOU reuse (`:207-248`) + the F-MS4-3 corpusRoot guard are LANDED; the wiring half stays U-MS2's (`mcp-server.ts:534-541` — the 3-arg pass-through feeding the store's `corpusRoot` + the `ImportStoreContext`).** | `docs/specs/multi-document-store-config-review.md` §2 D11 (four-agent gate, user go-ahead 2026-09-05) |
| **DECIDED: VECTOR-TOPOLOGY-PER-STORE — Phase-2 seams pinned NOW: per-store vector cache FILES, N boot controllers, per-store > global > lexical embedder precedence, non-default warm-up failure ⇒ that store stays lexical-pending permanently, registry embedder shape bans plaintext apiKeys** | 2026-09-05 (Phase-2-effective) | Pinned in the Phase-1 review so Phase 2 needs no rework (NOT implemented in Phase 1 — the parked row is `docs/pending.md`): (a) cache topology = PER-STORE files, one `createVectorCache({ path })` per store (`vector-cache.ts:170-174`), default `provident-vector-cache-<name>.json` with name `main` → the legacy `provident-vector-cache.json`; each store's §5.13 prune stays store-local — a shared single file would let one store's promotion prune other stores' entries (the critique's B1). (b) N `createVectorBootController(store, …)` instances (already store-scoped, `vector-boot.ts:110-114`). (c) Precedence: per-store `embedder` config > global env/args config (`main.ts:53-83`) > lexical; a store with no embedder config in global vector mode INHERITS the global config. (d) Warm-up: only the DEFAULT store's warm-up is sync-before-window (class 2 unchanged, `main.ts:159`); non-default stores warm up CONCURRENTLY in the background — a non-default warm-up failure ⇒ that store stays lexical-pending permanently (logged once, never retried), voiding the N-sequential-dark-boot cost. (e) Registry-file embedder shape: `Omit<EmbeddingProviderConfig,'apiKey'> & { apiKeyEnv?: string }` (`embeddings.ts:36-47` base) — a raw apiKey in the registry file is REJECTED by validation; secrets stay env-indirect. | `docs/specs/multi-document-store-config-review.md` §2 D6 (four-agent gate, user go-ahead 2026-09-05) |
| **DECIDED: UI-SELECTOR-DEFERRED — Phase 1 ships a read-only settings-pane store listing (provident-authored, operator-isolated scope, NOT MCP-visible) and NO active-store switcher; `RagQueryPayload` gains the SAME optional `store?: string` for MCP/UI mechanical symmetry; NO store-census MCP tool** | 2026-09-05 | The Phase-1 UI surface is a READ-ONLY store listing appended to the operator settings pane, authored as provident-ssr data in the isolated operator scope (PANE-PROVIDENT-AUTHORING + OPERATOR-ISOLATED-GRAPHSCOPE hold — a pane rendered outside the graph is a review finding), fed by a NEW read-only IPC channel + preload bridge method returning the registry's presentation view (name, default flag, persistence file name, corpus root, per-store loaded/corrupt status); NOT group-gated (IPC-SURFACE-NOT-GROUP-GATED, `decisions.md:45`) and NEVER MCP-visible. NO active-store switcher: the renderer boots/edits ONE store (the default) — "the UI's active store" is out of scope. MCP-UI-EQUIVALENCE resolution: `RagQueryPayload` gains the SAME optional `store?: string` (`types.ts:406-409`) forwarded by `handleRagQueryIpc` so the surfaces stay mechanically symmetric, while the pane's own query path passes nothing (a documented asymmetry row, sibling of EDIT-COMMIT-RETURN-ASYMMETRY); non-default search results are display-only (A2). NO store-census MCP tool (an agent must not enumerate store names — B9; the census is the operator UI); unknown-store errors echo only the caller-supplied name (A9). The new IPC channel is NOT a five-seam gate seam — the spec must state that explicitly so a later agent does not "fix" the census gap with an MCP `rag.list_stores` tool. **LANDED HALF (2026-09-05, Unit U-MS5 — spec `docs/specs/unit-ms5-settings-listing.md`, 41 pass / 3 skip, full suite 2415 pass / 41 skip):** the LISTING half of this row landed — the read-only `provident:rag-store-listing` IPC + the `bridge.rag.stores()` method + the provident-authored `operator-rag-stores` settings-pane section + the `RagQueryPayload.store?: string` passthrough. The DEFERRAL — the active-store SWITCHER (a UI control that changes the rendered store) — remains DEFERRED (Phase 1 still boots/edits ONE store, the default; "the UI's active store" stays a declared non-concept). The five refinement rows below (STORE-LISTING-IPC through RAG-QUERY-STORE-DISPLAY-ASYMMETRY) are this row's landed sub-decisions. | `docs/specs/multi-document-store-config-review.md` §2 D9/D10 (four-agent gate, user go-ahead 2026-09-05); landed-by U-MS5 2026-09-05 |
| **DECIDED: STORE-LISTING-IPC — a NEW read-only renderer→main IPC channel `provident:rag-store-listing` returns the registry's presentation view** | 2026-09-05 (LANDED, Unit U-MS5) | The `IPC_RAG_STORE_LISTING = 'provident:rag-store-listing'` channel (`shared/types.ts`) + the `handleRagStoreListingIpc` shared handler (`mcp-server.ts`, exported for direct unit testing) + the `ipcMain.handle` registration (`main.ts`) + the `bridge.rag.stores()` preload method — the registry's presentation view (one `RagStoreListingEntry` per configured store: name, default flag, persistence file NAME (basename — F7), corpus root, per-store load status). Manual-UI ONLY — NEVER MCP-visible (an agent cannot enumerate the configured store names, B9/A9). NOT group-gated (IPC-SURFACE-NOT-GROUP-GATED); NOT a five-seam gate seam (no `RpcMethod`, no `TOOL_GROUPS` row, no `ALL_TOOLS` row, no `MUTATING_METHODS` member, no renderer-switch method). The `RagQueryPayload.store?: string` passthrough rides this unit. | `docs/specs/unit-ms5-settings-listing.md` §4/§5.1/§5.2 (the §5.11-row that lands with the unit) |
| **DECIDED: STORE-LISTING-PROVIDENT-AUTHORED — the settings-pane listing is authored as provident-ssr data inside `settingsContent()`'s section** | 2026-09-05 (LANDED, Unit U-MS5) | The listing (`operator-rag-stores` container + `h3: 'RAG stores'` + one entry `div` per store or a placeholder `p`) is authored as provident-ssr data inside `settingsContent()`'s `section` (`sidebar-panes.ts`) and mounts in the isolated operator scope (PANE-PROVIDENT-AUTHORING + OPERATOR-ISOLATED-GRAPHSCOPE + UI-MOUNT-OPERATOR). NO handlers on any listing node (read-only; NO switcher); NO new `registerHandlerDef` (the handler-def census stays 12). A listing rendered outside the provident graph or inside the app graph is a review finding. | `docs/specs/unit-ms5-settings-listing.md` §4/§5.5 |
| **DECIDED: STORE-LISTING-BOOT-CACHED — the host fetches the listing ONCE at boot into `lastStoreListing` and re-renders it from the cache** | 2026-09-05 (LANDED, Unit U-MS5) | `boot()` fetches the listing once (`bridge.rag.stores()`) into the host's `lastStoreListing` cache BEFORE `mountOperator()`; the channel handler projects the IN-MEMORY boot-loaded registry form, never the registry file (D8 — a mid-run registry edit is observed only after restart). `reDerive()`/`refresh()` do NOT re-fetch (a red test pins the `stores()` call count stays 1 across a re-derive). A boot fetch bridge error does NOT abort boot (a display-only surface; `null` → the '(stores unavailable)' placeholder; deliberately DIVERGES from the snapshot/docHeads/template abort discipline — the F-MS5-3 awaited-inline availability note). | `docs/specs/unit-ms5-settings-listing.md` §4/§5.5 |
| **DECIDED: STORE-LISTING-STATUS-SHARED — `RagStoreLoadStatus` is declared ONCE in `shared/types.ts`; its three members are exactly the D7 failed-store matrix** | 2026-09-05 (LANDED, Unit U-MS5) | `RagStoreLoadStatus = 'loaded' | 'failed-corrupt' | 'failed-missing'` is the SINGLE shared declaration (`shared/types.ts`), coordinated with U-MS2's per-store `storeLoadStatus` derivation (`rag-store-directory.ts`) — a divergent re-declaration is a review finding (the A3/RCA-6 drift class, applied proactively). | `docs/specs/unit-ms5-settings-listing.md` §4/§5.1 |
| **DECIDED: RAG-QUERY-STORE-DISPLAY-ASYMMETRY — the MCP `rag.query` tool and the `rag-query` IPC are mechanically symmetric on the OPTIONAL `store` field, while the renderer's OWN query path never passes one** | 2026-09-05 (LANDED, Unit U-MS5) | `RagQueryPayload.store?: string` is forwarded by `handleRagQueryIpc` (`store: payload?.store` — the conditional-spread preload `query(query, topK?, store?)` idiom, byte-equal); the MCP tool + the IPC see the identical args shape (MCP-UI-EQUIVALENCE). The renderer's OWN query path (`submitQuery`) NEVER passes a store (no switcher; two-arg call) — a non-default result, if ever rendered in the search pane, is DISPLAY-ONLY: the search rows carry NO handler, and a `<name>:`-prefixed id does not resolve in the default store's document view. No UI control produces a non-default store in Phase 1. Sibling of EDIT-COMMIT-RETURN-ASYMMETRY (`decisions.md:46`). | `docs/specs/unit-ms5-settings-listing.md` §4/§5.6 (risk R9) |
| **DECIDED: RESULT-LEVEL-PROVENANCE — every `rag.query`/`rag-stream` result carries `citations` (the deduplicated grounding set `[{documentId, nodeId}]`) and `trace` (per-mode); a result without a `trace` is not a valid `RagResult`** | 2026-09-08 | The `rag.query`/`rag-stream` result is extended with `citations` + `trace` (flat mode = `{mode:'flat', engine, topK, source}`; graph mode = the ordered `reference`→`fact` path walked as `GraphTraceEntry[]`), plus the contract's `results` array and `blockedBy?`. The existing `ranked`/`context`/`markdown`/`lineMap`/`k`/`store` fields are PRESERVED (a backward-compatible additive extension). A result without a `trace` fails with `TraceUnavailable` (FS-16). | `docs/specs/unit-x-rag-provenance-traversal.md` §4 |
| **DECIDED: QUERY-AUDIT-LOG — every `ragQuery`/`ragStream` call is recorded in a bounded in-memory audit log (`getQueryAuditLog` → `[{query, filters, mode, resultCount, timestamp, requester}]`), exposed via BOTH the MCP `get_query_audit_log` tool AND the GUI audit panel (D4 parity)** | 2026-09-08 | A NEW pure module `src/main/query-audit.ts` hosts a bounded ring-buffer audit log (default 1000 entries; `record`/`list`/`clear`; never throws on a full log — the OLDEST entry is dropped). The log is created ONCE in main and shared by the MCP `rag.query`/`rag-stream` handlers + the `rag-query` IPC + the `get_query_audit_log` tool (the `handleRagTool` optional `auditLog?` param, threaded through `handleRagQueryIpc`). The GUI audit panel reads the SAME log via a `rag-query-audit` IPC (MCP/UI equivalence, §8.2). | `docs/specs/unit-x-rag-provenance-traversal.md` §4 |
| **DECIDED: GRAPH-MODE-WALK — `mode: 'graph'` is a deterministic, hop-limited local walk over the `reference`→`fact` graph (GAP-7, re-scoped to Astrographer — NOT an Incanter change)** | 2026-09-08 | `walkReferenceGraph` walks the `crosslink`-kind edges whose target is a `reference` OR `fact` node (a `content` target is NON-traversable), resolving through `FRESH`/`RESOLVED` and surfacing `BROKEN`/`STALE` in the trace (never silently traverses a stale embed). A `reference` target is a WAYPOINT (not a resolved target). A traversal that resolves no target returns an empty result with `blockedBy` (a valid state, not an error). `HopLimitExceeded` (FS-17) when the walk exceeds `maxHops` without resolving a target; `CycleDetected` (FS-18) on a `reference`→`fact` cycle. | `docs/specs/unit-x-rag-provenance-traversal.md` §4 |
| **DECIDED: PARENT-CONTEXT-EXPAND — `expand: 'parent'` returns the parent Document/node-cluster for a retrieved child, capped by `maxParentContext` (default 5) and stale-propagating** | 2026-09-08 | `expandParentContext` returns the top `maxParentContext` result items (by result order) with a `parent` (the owning Document's `title` + a `snippet`); items beyond the cap are returned WITHOUT a `parent`. A child with no owning document omits the `parent`. Stale-propagating: a child's `parent.stale` is `true` if ANY incoming `reference`→`fact` edge to the child has `state: 'STALE'`. Deterministic. | `docs/specs/unit-x-rag-provenance-traversal.md` §4 |
| **DECIDED: REFERENCE-GRAPH-ADDITIVE-FIELDS — the `reference`→`fact` graph is realized on the existing `RagStore` substrate via ADDITIVE fields (`RagNode.nodeKind?`, `RagEdge.edgeType?`, `RagEdge.state?`)** | 2026-09-08 | The `reference`→`fact` graph is the subgraph of `crosslink`-kind edges whose source is a `reference` node and target is a `fact` node, realized on the existing `RagStore` substrate via ADDITIVE fields: `RagNode.nodeKind?: 'content'|'fact'|'reference'` (default `'content'`), `RagEdge.edgeType?: 'link'|'embed'` (default `'link'`), and `RagEdge.state?: 'FRESH'|'RESOLVED'|'STALE'|'BROKEN'` (default `'RESOLVED'` for `link`, `'FRESH'` for `embed`). These follow the CHILDREN-ADDITIVE-STORE-FORMAT precedent: additive, hash-covered (in `nodeSource`/`edgeSource`), no migration/re-hash; validated at write (the byte-pinned `rag putNode: nodeKind required/invalid` / `rag putEdge: edgeType/state required/invalid`), skipped at boot. The runtime sets (`RAG_NODE_KINDS`/`RAG_EDGE_TYPES`/`RAG_REFERENCE_STATES`) live in `rag-store.ts`, not `adjacency.ts`. | `docs/specs/unit-x-rag-provenance-traversal.md` §4 |
| **DECIDED: QUALIFY-RESULT — the merged fan-out result is store-qualified by a PURE builder `qualifyStoreResult(merged, stores, { qualified })`; `opts.qualified` is the A-F1 gate (`true` stamps `store`/builds `storeContexts`; `false` returns `merged` byte-equal)** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-F2) | U-F2's pure builder (`src/main/retrieval.ts`) — PURE + DETERMINISTIC, no Electron/I/O, never mutates `merged` or the per-store results (`qualified:true` spread-copies items). `qualified:false` returns `merged` UNCHANGED (object-/byte-equal, `stores` not read) — the A-F1 gate that keeps the Phase-1 A4 single-store `ragQuery` provably byte-equal. `qualified:true` attributes each merged item to its producing store by REFERENCE IDENTITY against `stores[].result.results` (U-F1's `mergeStoreResults` interleaves by reference), re-derives `citations` with the first-appearance store, and builds `storeContexts`. Fail-states pinned (§5.6/§5.9): `qualifyStoreResult: merged result required` / `opts required` / `qualified must be a boolean` / `stores required when qualified` / `store entry required` / `store name must be a non-empty string` / `store result must have context, markdown and lineMap` (F-F2-1 adds `store results must be an array`) / `unattributable result item`. | `docs/specs/unit-f2-result-qualification.md` §4/§5 |
| **DECIDED: STORECONTEXTS-BLOCKS — the qualified result carries an ADDITIVE `storeContexts` array (one block per store with a VALID result, in registry-insertion order, default block FIRST; failed/null-result stores contribute NO block)** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-F2) | `StoreContextBlock = { store: string; context: RagNode[]; markdown: string; lineMap: LineNodeMap }` (D2). Each block carries that store's OWN assembled context/markdown/lineMap; the TOP-LEVEL `context`/`markdown`/`lineMap` on the `RagResult` stay the DEFAULT store's block (U-F1's merged block, UNCHANGED). `storeContexts` is absent (`undefined`) when NOT qualified (A-F1). A store with a VALID result but an EMPTY `results` array still contributes a (possibly empty) block — NOT an error; a null/undefined-result store (failed, D6) is SKIPPED. | `docs/specs/unit-f2-result-qualification.md` §4/§5.4/§5.5 |
| **DECIDED: STORE-FIELD-NAMING — every `store` value (per-item/per-entry/block) is the producing store's REGISTRY NAME, bare (no `<name>:` id prefix); the default store's `store` is its registry name (e.g. `'main'`), NEVER `''`** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-F2) | D3 — the `store` on every `RagResultItem`/`citations` entry/`storeContexts` block names the producing store's registry `name` WITHOUT the `<name>:` prefix (the prefix is a secondary signal only — a `'wiki'` store whose ids are `wiki:<id>` stamps `store:'wiki'`, NOT `'wiki:'`). A fan-out always runs under a registry (A-F3), so the default store's `store` is its registry name (`'main'`), NEVER `''` — the legacy `''` sentinel is UNREACHABLE in a qualified result. The additive `store?` on `GraphTraceEntry`/`BlockedByEntry` is TYPE-SHAPE UNIFORMITY ONLY — NEVER stamped by the qualified FLAT builder (the fan-out is FLAT-only, D4). | `docs/specs/unit-f2-result-qualification.md` §4/§5.4 |
| **DECIDED: FANOUT-INTERLEAVE-MERGE — the cross-store `stores:"all"` fan-out merges per-store top-k results by a pure, rank-based interleave (NEVER score-compare), skipping failed/empty stores, deterministic in registry-insertion order; the additive result-shape changes are A-F1-gated to U-F2** | 2026-09-08 | The cross-store fan-out slice is a 3-unit decomposition (D8: U-F1 pure merge → U-F2 store-qualification → U-F3 fan-out wiring). **D1 (merge policy, U-F1):** the merged `results` is a per-store top-k round-robin by rank (`mergeStoreResults(stores, { topK })`, `src/main/merge-store-results.ts`); NEVER a cross-store score comparison — a per-store `score > 0` floor is applied upstream and NOT re-applied, making mixed embedders SAFE; merged length ≤ N×topK. **D6 (skip-failed/empty, U-F1):** an empty-`results` store contributes zero; a NON-default store whose `ragQuery` threw is passed as `{ name, result: null }` and SKIPPED (never a loud fail); a FAILED DEFAULT store (first entry) FAILS LOUD (`mergeStoreResults: default store result required`) — the deliberate D6 default-exemption, since the top-level `ranked`/`context`/`markdown`/`lineMap`/`k` block is the default store's. **D7 (determinism, U-F1):** the merge consumes stores in canonical (registry insert order = `RagStoreDirectory.entries` `Map` key order); deterministic (same input → same output). **A-F1 (gating):** the `storeContexts`/per-item `store` shape changes are U-F2's, gated to `stores:"all"` ONLY; U-F1 keeps the top-level fields as the default store's block and does NOT touch `RagResult`/`RagResultItem` (Phase-1 A4 single-store byte-equality untouched). **LANDED (2026-09-08, Unit U-F1 — spec `docs/specs/unit-f1-merge-store-results.md` §3a/§4/§5, greens 23 PASS / 0 FAIL, `tests/unit-f1-merge-store-results.test.ts` 31/31):** the pure module exported `mergeStoreResults` + `StoreResultInput` (`src/main/merge-store-results.ts`); adversarial F-F1-1..F-F1-4 all HOST — F-F1-2/F-F1-3 FIXED + regression-tested (the default-store block-field assertion with the byte-pinned `…must carry ranked/context/markdown/lineMap/k`, and the sparse-array/`null`-element interleave skip), F-F1-1/F-F1-4 INFO (documented, no fix — string-coercion dedup key + by-reference item copy, per spec §5.2). U-F2 (`docs/specs/unit-f2-result-qualification.md`) + U-F3 (`docs/specs/unit-f3-stores-all-schema.md`) NOT yet landed. |  `archive/gate-reviews/2026-09-21-multi-store-fanout-review.md` (historical; archived 2026-09-21; successor docs/decisions.md#FANOUT-INTERLEAVE-MERGE + docs/specs/unit-f1-merge-store-results.md … unit-f3-stores-all-schema.md) §2 D1-D8 + §3 A-F1 (four-agent gate, user go-ahead given 2026-09-08) |

| **DECIDED: STORES-ALL-SCHEMA — `stores?: 'all'` (a single-member string enum) is added to the `rag.query`/`rag-stream` inputSchemas + the `rag-query` IPC `RagQueryPayload`, ADDITIVE alongside (never replacing) the existing optional `store`** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-F3) | D5/A-F4 — `rag.query`/`rag-stream`: `stores: z.enum(['all']).optional()`; `RagQueryPayload.stores?: 'all'`. Omission keeps the single-store path byte-equal. Mutually exclusive with `store` (A-F3). A direct caller passing a non-`'all'` `stores` value fails the defensive pin `rag.query: stores must be "all"` (mirrors the zod enum). | `docs/specs/unit-f3-stores-all-schema.md` §4/§5.1 |
| **DECIDED: REGISTRY-REQUIRED — `stores:"all"` with `dir == null`/undefined (no registry to fan across) FAILS LOUD before any engine call (A-F3)** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-F3) | The legacy directory-less single-store sentinel has no registry to fan out to. `{ stores:'all' }` with a null/omitted `dir` throws `rag.query: stores:"all" requires a configured store registry` (rejects identically on the `rag-query` IPC). Precedes the engine call. | `docs/specs/unit-f3-stores-all-schema.md` §4/§5.2/§5.9 |
| **DECIDED: MUTUAL-EXCLUSION — a request carrying BOTH `store` AND `stores` is rejected before any engine call (A-F3); the guard fires BEFORE `resolveStoreArg`'s unknown-store M2 throw** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-F3) | Guard precedence (arbitration): `{ store: anything, stores }` → `rag.query: store and stores are mutually exclusive`, never the M2 unknown-store throw. In `rag-stream` it surfaces as the `[{type:'error',error}]` chunk. Byte-pinned message. | `docs/specs/unit-f3-stores-all-schema.md` §4/§5.2/§5.9 |
| **DECIDED: FLAT-ONLY-GUARD — `stores:"all"` with `mode:'graph'` FAILS LOUD (byte-pinned `rag.query: stores:"all" is only valid in flat mode`)** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-F3) | Consumed from D4: the fan-out is FLAT-mode only. `mode:'graph'` WITHOUT `stores:"all"` is unchanged (a valid single-store graph query). The guard is the last of the four §5.2 `stores:"all"` guards (after mutual-exclusion, stores-value, dir==null). | `docs/specs/unit-f3-stores-all-schema.md` §4/§5.2/§5.9 |

| **DECIDED: REGISTRY-ATOMIC-WRITE — every registry persist is atomic (`mkdirSync(recursive)` → `writeFileSync(path+'.tmp')` → fsync the temp → `renameSync`); a failed write leaves the ORIGINAL file intact** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-H1) | Persisted form is `JSON.stringify({ version: 1, stores }, null, 2)`. SUPERSEDES the boot-time-only "registry change observed only after restart" assertion for this module and — by A-P2-3 designation — `tests/unit-ms5-settings-listing.test.ts` Red 18 (the DESIGNATION pins here; the mechanical refresh-on-apply wiring + the Red 18 re-write land in U-H2 as the runtime controller's job). | **DECIDED: REGISTRY-WRITE-FAILS-LOUD — a persist/write failure THROWS (the native fs Error), deliberately diverging from the sibling stores' catch-and-swallow** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-H1) | An unobservable hot-write failure would let persisted-and-live diverge silently (violating D2). mkdir/writeFileSync/fsync/renameSync throwing ⇒ the native fs Error PROPAGATES (EACCES/EROFS/ENOENT/EISDIR/ENOTEMPTY/ENOSPC/…) — the throw path is pinned, the message is the native one (vs the siblings' `catch {}` at `module-store.ts:139-141`/`template-store.ts:108-110`, NOT copied). The write module emits ZERO console.* log output (failures are thrown, not logged; the controller decides how to surface them). | `docs/specs/unit-h1-registry-write.md` §4/§5.4/§5.5 |
| **DECIDED: REGISTRY-DELTA-RETURN — the write module returns the new registry state AND the delta `{ added | removed | renamed }`; the delta is a pure structural computation, exactly ONE non-empty member per mutation** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-H1) | Applying the delta to the live runtime — teardown, engine construct, re-bind of the const-captured closures, the `teardown()` primitives — is the RUNTIME controller's (U-H2) explicitly NOT this unit's job. `add` → `added:[name]`; `remove` (orphan, D3 — the entry only, the persistence file + journal NEVER touched) → `removed:[name]`; `rename` (NON-default only, D4/D5; the no-persisted-ids half is a documented U-H2 caller precondition) → `renamed:[{from,to}]`. | `docs/specs/unit-h1-registry-write.md` §4/§5.4/§5.6 |
| **DECIDED: RUNTIME-CONTROLLER — a NEW node-testable module `src/main/rag-store-runtime.ts` exports `createRagStoreRuntimeController(opts)`, a controller owning the mutable live directory + the single hot-apply orchestration seam** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-H2a module 38/38) | The controller is CONSTRUCTED ONCE at boot AROUND the boot `RagStoreDirectory` (the SAME object `buildRagStoreDirectory` built) — construction is side-effect-free: NO rebuild, NO fs, NO re-read (presence is byte-equal to today's boot). The factory + 3 types (`RagStoreRuntimeOptions`/`HotApplyResult`/`RagStoreRuntimeController`) + 10 controller methods (`getDirectory`/`getDefaultEntry`/`getDefaultName`/`getDefaultStore`/`getDefaultEngine`/`getVectorBoot`/`getRegistryPath`/`currentStores`/`statusOf`/`hotApply`). | `docs/specs/unit-h2-runtime-controller.md` §4/§5.1–§5.4 |
| **DECIDED: MUTABLE-LIVE-DIRECTORY — the controller holds the boot `RagStoreDirectory` and mutates its `entries` Map IN PLACE (never replacing the Map or the directory object)** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-H2a module 38/38) | `entries` stays typed `ReadonlyMap` (consumers cannot write) but the runtime object is a real `Map`; the controller is the ONLY writer. | **DECIDED: ATOMIC-APPLY — `hotApply` order (D7): validate → (rename) D4 no-ids inspection → `writeRegistryMutation` (the only disk touch, atomic; a throw leaves disk+live untouched) → CONSTRUCT-fully-then-INSERT into a STAGING map (if any construct throws, the live Map is NOT mutated) → swap in ONE synchronous pass → return `{ loaded, delta }`** | 2026-09-08 (LANDED, Unit U-H2a module 38/38) | A successful write ALWAYS completes the swap for U-H2's reachable set (loader-validated ⇒ non-throwing constructs), preserving D2 (persisted-and-live never diverge); the (unreachable) construct-throw case is surfaced by the throw, not silent divergence. **AMENDED 2026-09-08 (HOST-1, with the U-H2a module):** when the post-write default-drift defensive checks fire (`R-default-changed`/`R-default-mismatch2` — external mid-run registry default edit), the live directory is SYNCHRONIZED to the fresh `loaded` BEFORE the throw (D2 preserved even in the throw path; this sync-before-throw is the ONLY throwing path that mutates the live Map). | `docs/specs/unit-h2-runtime-controller.md` §4/§5.4/§5.7 |
